We present an integrated, electromagnetic tracking (EMT)-based navigation system for femoral osteotomies that minimizes dissection and intraoperative fluoroscopy. The system couples CT-based preoperative planning with one-time intraoperative C-arm calibration and accurate X-ray-to-CT registration from two fluoroscopic images acquired at initialization. This enables real-time, fluoroscopy-free EMT navigation of the saw blade and bone fragments relative to the preoperative plan, and is compatible with uniplanar and biplanar osteotomies. In a feasibility study using 18 synthetic femora, EMT guidance significantly outperformed free-hand execution in total angular error ($(3.05 \pm 0.75)^\circ$ vs.\ $(6.32 \pm 2.36)^\circ$, $p=0.031$), assuming the same minimal surgical exposure for both. No EMT-guided trials exceeded the >5° clinical threshold, whereas free-hand produced 4 outliers of 6 trials. The system achieved statistical equivalence ($\pm 2^\circ$, $\pm 2,\text{mm}$) to PSI for total angular ($p \le 0.02$) and total translational ($p=0.048$) errors, with no significant differences in user questionnaire scores. By transferring preoperative plans using only two fluoroscopic images while matching PSI accuracy without additional surgical exposure, the proposed system motivates subsequent cadaveric and clinical validation.

</details>


### [108] [An Attention-Based Denoising Model for Diffusion Weighted Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03903)
*Prithviraj Verma,Pawan Kumar,Chandan Deshani,Prasun Chandra Tripathi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于噪声感知注意力机制的去噪框架，结合层级Swin Transformer窗口注意力和多维门控精化模块，用于低采集时间的弥散加权成像（DWI）恢复。该方法在1%-15%噪声水平下达到平均PSNR 33.69 dB、SSIM 0.8539，展现了鲁棒的去噪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DWI用于全身癌症筛查时采集时间长，缩短扫描时间会导致图像质量下降、噪声增加。幅度重建引入的Rician噪声具有信号依赖性，传统卷积方法难以有效去噪。

Method: 提出噪声感知注意力去噪框架，包含三个关键组件：1）层级Swin Transformer窗口注意力机制进行上下文建模；2）基于Transformer的多维门控精化模块进行通道自适应细化；3）显式噪声水平条件化与残差重建，实现异方差噪声的自适应抑制。

Result: 在1%至15%噪声水平下，模型达到平均PSNR 33.69 dB、SSIM 0.8539，且在严重噪声条件下保持稳定性能。

Conclusion: 注意力引导的上下文建模结合通道自适应精化，为DWI去噪提供了一种鲁棒且泛化性强的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion-weighted imaging (DWI) is used for whole-body cancer screening, but it typically requires a long acquisition time. When the scan time is reduced, the image quality often suffers, leading to increased noise in the scans. Magnitude reconstruction in DWI introduces signal-dependent Rician noise, which makes denoising more challenging for conventional convolution-based methods. To address this limitation, we propose a noise-aware attention-driven denoising framework that integrates hierarchical Swin Transformer window attention with transformer-based multi-dimensional gated refinement for DWI restoration. The model incorporates explicit noise-level conditioning and residual reconstruction to enable adaptive suppression of heteroscedastic noise across a wide range of corruption levels. Experimental evaluation on corrupted DWI scans demonstrates strong restoration performance. Our model achieves a mean PSNR of 33.69~dB and SSIM of 0.8539 across noise levels from 1\% to 15\%, while maintaining stable behavior under severe noise conditions. These results indicate that attention-guided contextual modeling combined with channel-adaptive refinement provides a robust and generalizable solution for DWI denoising.

</details>


### [109] [SparseStreet: Sparse Gaussian Splatting for Real-Time Street Scene Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03909)
*Qingpo Wuwu,Xiaobao Wei,Peng Chen,Nan Huang,Zhongyu Zhao,Hao Wang,Ming Lu,Ningning Ma,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对3D高斯泼溅在街景重建中存储开销大、渲染速度慢的问题，提出SparseStreet压缩框架，通过节点可学习剪枝和背景压缩，在Waymo和nuScenes上实现高达80%的压缩率且质量损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D高斯泼溅街景重建方法需要大量高斯基元来捕捉精细细节，导致存储成本高昂和渲染速度缓慢。动态物体需要高保真表示以保持时间一致性，而静态背景区域存在大量冗余。

Method: 提出SparseStreet通用压缩框架：1) 基于节点的可学习剪枝策略，系统性地移除低贡献高斯基元同时保留视觉关键区域；2) 场景表示稳定后应用背景压缩，进一步减少静态区域冗余。

Result: 在Waymo和nuScenes数据集上，SparseStreet实现了高达80%的压缩比，且质量退化最小，实现了资源高效的高保真动态场景重建。

Conclusion: SparseStreet能够有效保留动态物体的几何和外观，同时显著减少高斯基元总数，为资源受限场景下的高保真街景重建提供了有效方案。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting has shown promising results in street scene reconstruction, existing methods require massive numbers of Gaussian primitives to capture fine details, leading to prohibitive storage costs and slow rendering speeds. We observe that dynamic objects (e.g., vehicles and pedestrians) demand high-fidelity representations to maintain temporal consistency, while static background regions often contain substantial redundancy. Motivated by this, we propose SparseStreet, a general compression framework specifically designed for street scenes. First, we introduce a node-based learnable pruning strategy that systematically removes low-contributing Gaussian primitives while preserving visually critical regions. Second, after the scene representation stabilizes, we apply background compression, further reducing redundancy in static regions. Our method effectively preserves the geometry and appearance of dynamic objects while significantly reducing the total number of Gaussian primitives. Extensive experiments on the Waymo and nuScenes demonstrate that SparseStreet achieves up to 80% compression ratio with minimal quality degradation, enabling resource-efficient, high-fidelity dynamic scene reconstruction. Project website: https://sparsestreet.github.io/.

</details>


### [110] [Bootstrap Your Generator: Unpaired Visual Editing with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03911)
*Yoad Tewel,Yuval Atzmon,Gal Chechik,Lior Wolf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ByG框架，利用冻结生成模型自身知识进行无配对训练的流匹配编辑模型，通过梯度路由和循环一致性实现高质量图像视频编辑，无需外部监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型编辑训练依赖大量配对数据，视频编辑中配对数据收集成本极高，限制了可扩展性。

Method: 从冻结的基础模型中提取指令跟随线索，结合循环一致性保持结构；提出梯度路由机制，将下游干净预测的损失梯度回传至噪声训练状态，弥合训练-推理差距。

Result: 在数据稀缺的图像和视频编辑场景中实现了最先进结果，泛化到未见领域，性能超越在百万样本上训练的监督基线。

Conclusion: 梯度路由桥接了训练-推理差距；从基础模型提取语义线索提供了鲁棒的训练信号，无需外部奖励模型。

Abstract: Modern generative models possess a deep understanding of visual content, yet training them for image editing typically requires massive datasets of paired examples. This limits scalability, especially for video editing where collecting paired data is prohibitively expensive. We propose Bootstrap Your Generator (ByG), a general framework for unpaired training of flow matching editing models. It leverages the base model's knowledge without any external signal. Our approach pairs instruction-following cues extracted from the frozen model with cycle-consistency for structure preservation. To make this tractable, we propose to route gradients from downstream losses over clean predictions to noisy training states. We demonstrate state-of-the-art results on challenging data-scarce image and video editing scenarios. Extensive evaluations and user studies show that our method effectively generalizes to unseen domains and outperforms supervised baselines trained on millions of samples. Analysis reveals that our gradient routing bridges the train-inference gap, and extracting semantic cues from a base model provides a robust training signal that obviates the need for external reward models.

</details>


### [111] [PatchScene: Patch-based Voxel Diffusion for Large-Scale Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03915)
*Qingdong Xu,Jiajun Zhu,Shilin Zhu,Xinjing He,Chao Lu,Huanran Wang,Jiyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PatchScene，一种基于扩散的LiDAR场景补全新框架。采用基于块的点素扩散范式生成局部细粒度几何，通过置信度引导的时空融合机制整合重叠块与相邻帧，并利用环形流扩散策略从近到远传播高保真信息，实现空间无界场景补全。在SemanticKITTI上达到SOTA，且20m训练模型可直接泛化至50m场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模LiDAR场景补全方法依赖全局潜在表征或稠密体素网格，难以同时保证局部几何精细度与长距离场景的一致性重建。

Method: (1) 基于块的体素扩散范式，在局部3D区域显式生成细粒度几何；(2) 置信度引导的时空融合机制，统一整合重叠块与相邻帧；(3) 环形流扩散策略，利用LiDAR径向密度模式，从近到远渐进传播高保真信息，实现空间无界补全。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI基准上所有标准指标达到SOTA，几何精度和时间一致性均超越先前方法。20m范围训练的模型无需微调即可有效泛化至50m场景。

Conclusion: PatchScene通过局部生成与全局融合结合的设计，在LiDAR场景补全上实现了优异的精度、时空一致性和跨距离泛化能力，适用于真实自动驾驶应用。

Abstract: We propose PatchScene, a novel diffusion-based framework for large-scale LiDAR scene completion. Unlike existing methods that rely on global latent representations or dense voxel grids, PatchScene adopts a patch-based voxel diffusion paradigm that explicitly generates fine-grained geometry within localized 3D regions. To ensure coherent reconstruction at both spatial and temporal scales, we introduce a confidence-guided spatio-temporal fusion mechanism that integrates overlapping patches and adjacent frames in a unified generative process. Furthermore, we design an Annular-Flow diffusion strategy that leverages the radial density pattern of LiDAR scans to progressively propagate high-fidelity information from near-range to far-range regions, enabling spatially unbounded scene completion. Extensive experiments on the SemanticKITTI benchmark demonstrate that PatchScene achieves state-of-the-art performance across all standard metrics, surpassing previous approaches in both geometric accuracy and temporal consistency. Remarkably, the model trained on 20 m LiDAR ranges generalizes effectively to 50 m scenes without retraining, highlighting its strong scalability and generalization capability for real-world autonomous driving applications.

</details>


### [112] [Benchmarking Visual State Tracking in Multimodal Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03920)
*Sihyun Yu,Nanye Ma,Pinzhi Huang,Hyunseok Lee,Shusheng Yang,June Suk Choi,Ellis Brown,Oscar Michel,Boyang Zheng,Jinwoo Shin,Saining Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VSTAT基准，用于评估多模态大模型在视频中的视觉状态追踪能力。发现当前最先进的MLLM在该任务上远低于人类水平，失败原因在于视觉感知而非文本推理，且现有代理方法也无法解决这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解评估多关注孤立时刻或片段级任务，而忽略了视觉状态追踪这一视频理解的基石能力。作者旨在填补这一评估空白，诊断MLLM在连续视频流中追踪实体状态和事件变化的真实能力。

Method: 构建VSTAT基准，包含834个来自合成和真实视频的片段，以及1500个需要跨帧连续感知和事件集成才能回答的问题。通过对比MLLM的思维链与视频流，分析其失败原因。

Result: 最先进的MLLM在VSTAT上的表现远低于人类，仅略高于基于先验答案的基线。MLLM在文本推理上能正确追踪，但在视觉感知所需事件上失败。基于MLLM的视频代理和编码代理也无法解决这一失败。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM在视频状态追踪任务上的核心瓶颈在于视觉感知环节，而非推理环节，现有代理方法同样不足以弥合这一差距，需要从根本上提升MLLM对连续视觉事件的感知能力。

Abstract: Understanding a video requires more than recognizing isolated moments, as humans continuously track entities, states, and events over time. This capacity for visual state tracking is fundamental to video understanding, yet remains underexplored in current evaluations of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). We introduce Visual STAte Tracking benchmark (VSTAT), a video-based benchmark designed to diagnose visual state tracking in MLLMs. VSTAT consists of 834 clips drawn from both synthetic and real-world videos, paired with 1,500 questions that cannot be answered from any single frame or short segment, requiring continuous perception and integration of events across the entire video stream. Despite their strong performance on existing video benchmarks, we find that state-of-the-art MLLMs perform far below humans and only modestly above answer-prior baselines. To analyze this gap, we compare MLLMs' thinking traces with the underlying video stream to understand why and when MLLMs fail on VSTAT. We find that MLLMs reason and track correctly in text, but fail at visually perceiving the events they need to track. Finally, our preliminary evaluation suggests that recent agentic approaches, including MLLM-based video agents and coding agents, do not readily resolve these failures, still falling short on VSTAT.

</details>


### [113] [GARDEN: Gravity-Aligned Reconstruction of Disentangled ENvironments from RGB images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03921)
*Jiahao Sun,Dingkun Wei,Zehong Shen,Hongyu Zhou,Yujun Shen,Liang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GARDEN是一个仅使用RGB多视角图像就能将场景重建为可仿真结构化混合表示（包含独立刚体+解耦背景）的框架，通过重力先验解决姿态模糊、恢复物体6-DoF位姿并分离背景几何，从而直接支持物理仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视角RGB重建管线输出的是整体式场景表示，缺乏显式物理结构，且存在任意全局旋转模糊、刚体前景与背景几何纠缠的问题，阻碍了稳定的物理交互；而依赖检索CAD模型替换的方案引入了缓慢的检索-替换阶段并削弱了几何保真度。

Method: ①重力对齐：将重建对齐到统一的Gravity-View坐标系以消除度量模糊；②物体中心网格恢复：恢复具有精确6-DoF位姿的刚体网格；③条件3D点分类：通过分类去除背景中的重复物体几何；最终得到解耦背景与显式刚体相结合的混合场景表示。

Result: 在模拟和真实多视角场景上的实验表明，GARDEN在物体放置可靠性、解开质量及渲染-仿真效率方面均优于基于检索的基线方法。

Conclusion: 利用重力作为通用物理先验，将重建问题重新表述为物理接地场景因子分解，能够在不依赖CAD检索的前提下实现可直接用于物理仿真的结构化场景表示，同时保持视觉真实性。

Abstract: Converting multi-view RGB observations into simulation-ready 3D environments remains challenging because current reconstruction pipelines produce monolithic scene representations without explicit physical structure. They are typically defined up to an arbitrary global rotation and entangle rigid foreground objects with background geometry, which hinders stable physical interaction. Existing solutions often recover interactivity by replacing reconstructed objects with retrieved CAD assets, but this introduces a slow retrieval-and-replacement stage and weakens scene-specific geometric fidelity. We propose GARDEN, an RGB-only framework that reformulates reconstruction as physically-grounded scene factorization and outputs a structured hybrid scene representation. The key idea is to use gravity as a universal physical prior: we first align the reconstruction to a unified Gravity-View frame to resolve gauge ambiguity, then recover object-centric rigid meshes with accurate 6-DoF placement, and finally remove duplicate object geometry from the background through conditional 3D point classification. The resulting representation combines explicit rigid bodies with a decoupled background, enabling direct physics simulation while preserving visual realism. Experiments on both simulated and real multi-view scenes show that GARDEN improves object placement reliability, disentanglement quality, and rendering-simulation efficiency compared with retrieval-based baselines.

</details>


### [114] [Adaptive Causal Alignment for High-Confidence Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03925)
*Zhiming Luo,Kejia Zhang,Yingxin Lai,Junwei Wu,Juanjuan Weng,Shaozi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了逆对抗训练中高置信度预测的悖论——高置信度往往源于对非因果背景特征的过拟合。提出HICAT框架，通过“测量-去偏-对齐”流水线，利用可学习背景偏置估计器（LBBE）自适应诊断上下文效用，结合自适应去偏机制和前景逻辑正交增强（FLOE）损失实现特征解耦，在多个基准数据集上有效缩小鲁棒泛化差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆对抗训练中高置信度预测存在悖论：高置信度常来自对非因果背景相关性的过拟合而非对象语义，而盲目抑制背景会导致严重特征损失（Feature Loss），亟需区分背景作为必要支持性先验与作为虚假混淆变量（spurious confounder）的双重角色。

Method: 提出HICAT框架，基于“Measure-Debias-Align”三阶段流水线：1）可学习背景偏置估计器（LBBE）自适应诊断上下文效用；2）自适应去偏机制进行外科手术式logit修正；3）前景逻辑正交增强（FLOE）损失，基于几何原理强制特征解耦。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNet-1K上，HICAT在CNN和ViT等多种架构上一致优于匹配基线，显著缩小了鲁棒泛化差距（robust generalization gap）。

Conclusion: 视觉上下文具有双重性，HICAT通过建立语义均衡（Semantic Equilibrium）能有效区分有益背景与虚假混淆，在不丢失关键特征的前提下提升鲁棒泛化能力。

Abstract: Inverse adversarial training leverages high-confidence predictions to stabilize robust learning, yet we uncover a critical paradox: high confidence often stems from overfitting to non-causal background correlations rather than intrinsic object semantics. Our investigation reveals that visual context functions as a dual-natured signal, serving as either a necessary supportive prior or a spurious confounder. This insight renders existing blind suppression strategies flawed, as they inevitably lead to severe Feature Loss. To resolve this, we propose High-Confidence Causally Aligned Training (HICAT), a unified framework that establishes a Semantic Equilibrium. Operating on a ``Measure-Debias-Align'' pipeline, HICAT integrates a Learnable Background-Bias Estimator (LBBE) to adaptively diagnose context utility. Guided by this diagnosis, an Adaptive Debiasing mechanism performs surgical logit rectification, complemented by a geometrically grounded Foreground Logit Orthogonal Enhancement (FLOE) loss to enforce rigorous feature disentanglement. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet-1K demonstrate that HICAT consistently improves over matched baselines across diverse architectures (CNNs and ViTs) while significantly reducing the robust generalization gap.

</details>


### [115] [Demo2Tutorial: From Human Experience to Multimodal Software Tutorials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03951)
*Zechen Bai,Zhiheng Chen,Yiqi Lin,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Difei Gao,Xiangwu Guo,Xin Wang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Demo2Tutorial是一个将人类在数字环境中的屏幕录制和交互日志转化为结构化多模态软件教程的框架，既能帮助人类学习，也能提升GUI智能体的规划能力，生成的教程质量超越人类编写的教程和基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字环境中的人类体验包含了丰富但未被充分利用的程序性知识，现有方法缺乏将这种非结构化体验转化为可重用、结构化教程的有效手段。

Method: 框架包含四个模块：1) 专用记录器收集屏幕录制和交互日志；2) 多模态Action Parser解析原始体验，重建感知、动作和意图；3) Step Planner将步骤抽象为层次化任务图；4) Tutorial Composer将解析后的体验转化为结构化的图文指令。

Result: 在官方软件文档构建的基准上，Demo2Tutorial生成的教程质量超过人类编写的教程，显著优于基线方法；同时实现了更快的人类任务完成速度和更优的GUI智能体规划能力。

Conclusion: 从人类体验中提取的结构化教程可以作为有效的知识表征，同时促进人类学习和智能体能力提升。

Abstract: Human experience in digital environments offers a vast, underexplored resource of authentic, untrimmed interactions that contain rich procedural knowledge. We introduce Demo2Tutorial, a framework that transforms this experience captured via screen recordings and interaction logs into structured, multimodal software tutorials for teaching both humans and agents. Demo2Tutorial first collects human experience via a dedicated recorder, then parses raw experience using a multimodal Action Parser to reconstruct perception, action, and intent. A Step Planner then abstracts these steps into hierarchical task graphs representing goals and steps. Finally, a Tutorial Composer transforms the parsed experience into structured, reusable image-text instructions. We evaluate the tutorial generation quality on a new benchmark derived from official software documentation. We further demonstrate that this distilled representation benefits (i) human learning, by automatically generating multimodal tutorials, and (ii) agent learning, by improving downstream GUI-agent planning and generalization. Experiments show Demo2Tutorial produces high-quality tutorials that surpass human-authored ones and significantly outperform baseline methods, while enabling both faster human task completion and improved GUI agent planning, demonstrating that structured tutorials distilled from human experience can serve as effective knowledge representations for advancing both human learning and agent capabilities. Code and data will be available at https://github.com/showlab/Demo2Tutorial.

</details>


### [116] [VLESA: Vision-Language Embodied Safety Agent for Human Activity Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03954)
*Hanjiang Hu,Yiyuan Pan,Jiaxing Li,Xusheng Luo,Alexander Robey,Na Li,Yebin Wang,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLESA是一个视觉-语言 embodied 安全代理框架，通过从自我中心视频监测人类活动，预测危险行为并实时触发安全干预。它引入了目标条件的安全Q-filter（通过GRPO训练），结合意图-动作预测代理，在ASIMOV-2.0基准上实现了更高的干预准确率，并将动作安全性提升超过41个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在物理任务中辅助人类时，物理动作具有不可逆的后果，因此需要确保安全。传统方法未考虑意图相关的安全性——相同动作在不同上下文中可能安全或危险。

Method: 提出VLESA框架，包含：(1) 配对自我中心视频帧与目标条件安全标注的数据集；(2) 通过GRPO训练的目标条件安全Q-filter，无需重训练即可评估动作与推断意图的关系；(3) 意图-动作预测代理，联合推断目标并预测未来动作。

Result: 在ASIMOV-2.0基准上，VLESA在精确地面真值帧处实现了比基线更高的干预准确率；GRPO训练的Q-filter通过目标条件约束解码将动作安全性提升了超过41个百分点。

Conclusion: VLESA有效地解决了意图相关的物理动作安全问题，通过目标条件安全评估和实时干预机制，显著提升了AI辅助物理任务的安全性。

Abstract: As AI systems increasingly assist humans in physical tasks, ensuring safety becomes paramount -- physical actions carry immediate and irreversible consequences that digital errors do not. We introduce the Vision-Language Embodied Safety Agent (VLESA), a framework that monitors human activities from egocentric video and triggers real-time safety interventions when dangerous actions are predicted. VLESA addresses intent-dependent safety where identical actions can be safe or dangerous depending on context. A dataset pairing egocentric frames with goal-conditioned safety annotations is introduced, enabling a goal-conditioned safety Q-filter trained via GRPO that evaluates actions with respect to inferred intent without retraining. On top of that, an intent-action prediction agent is proposed to jointly infer goals and predict future actions from video. On the ASIMOV-2.0 benchmark, VLESA achieves higher intervention accuracy at the exact ground-truth frame compared to baselines, while the GRPO-trained Q-filter improves action safety by over 41 percentage points through goal-conditioned constrained decoding. Code is available at https://github.com/HanjiangHu/VLESA.

</details>


### [117] [Video-Mirai: Autoregressive Video Diffusion Models Need Foresight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03971)
*Yonghao Yu,Lang Huang,Runyi Li,Zerun Wang,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Video-Mirai，一种仅训练阶段的方法，通过冻结的未来感知编码器将非因果的未来信息蒸馏到因果状态中，弥合视频自回归扩散模型中的表征级规划缺口，在不改变推理时架构、计算量和KV缓存的情况下，显著提升长视频生成的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准流式自回归视频扩散训练仅要求每个因果状态解释当前片段，导致模型丢弃了保持未来一致性所需的身份、布局和运动信息，即表征级规划缺口。

Method: 在训练时，生成器因果地生成完整展开序列，冻结的未来感知编码器以非因果方式读取完整序列，轻量级预测器将得到的停止梯度目标蒸馏到因果状态中；推理时丢弃编码器和预测器，保持原始架构不变。

Result: 在5秒VBench上，总分从83.8提升至84.6；30秒超训练时长展开中，主体一致性从84.9提至88.5，背景一致性从90.2提至91.9。

Conclusion: 因果性应约束推理而非表征监督，视觉自回归模型需要具备前瞻能力。

Abstract: Causal video generators must predict from the past, but they need not learn only from it. In streaming autoregressive video diffusion, each emitted segment becomes a commitment that future segments must preserve. Standard training, however, only asks each causal state to explain the present. This creates what we call a representation-level planning gap: states that fit the current segment may discard identity, layout, and motion information needed for a consistent future. We introduce Video-Mirai, a training-only method that closes this gap without changing causal inference: the generator rolls out causally, a frozen foresight encoder reads the completed rollout non-causally, and a lightweight predictor distills the resulting stopped-gradient targets into causal states. Future frames supervise representations, never generator inputs. At inference, the encoder and predictor are discarded, leaving the original architecture, per-step FLOPs, and KV-cache behavior unchanged. Video-Mirai improves a strong Causal-Forcing baseline on 5-second VBench from 83.8 to 84.6 in terms of Total Score. On 30-second rollouts beyond the training horizon, subject consistency improves from 84.9 to 88.5 and background consistency from 90.2 to 91.9. Ablations identify future-conditioned targets as the key ingredient, and probes show that future frames become more decodable from current features. Causality should constrain inference, not representation supervision. Our study highlights that visual autoregressive models need foresight. Project page: https://y0uroy.github.io/Video-Mirai.

</details>


### [118] [AAD-1: Asymmetric Adversarial Distillation for One-Step Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03972)
*Haobo Li,Yanhong Zeng,Yunhong Lu,Jiapeng Zhu,Hao Ouyang,Qiuyu Wang,Ka Leong Cheng,Yujun Shen,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AAD-1提出了一种非对称对抗蒸馏框架，用于单步自回归图像到视频生成，通过打破生成器与判别器之间的对称性（生成器保持因果性，判别器双向关注全局时空上下文）以及分阶段训练策略（先分布匹配预热，再对抗蒸馏），解决了运动坍缩和训练不稳定性问题，在VBench上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进方法采用对抗蒸馏进行自回归视频生成，但存在运动坍缩（motion collapse）和训练不稳定性问题，导致生成静态视频。AAD-1旨在解决这些挑战。

Method: 提出非对称对抗蒸馏框架：1) 架构上打破生成器与判别器的对称性——生成器保持因果性以保留自回归采样能力，判别器则双向关注完整时空上下文，为整个视频序列输出单一的整体真实度评分；2) 训练上采用分阶段策略，先使用分布匹配引导出一个稳定的单步生成器作为预热阶段，将学生分布拉近教师分布，再进行对抗蒸馏。

Result: 在VBench上的大量实验表明，AAD-1在单步自回归视频生成中达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 非对称设计与分阶段训练策略有效解决了自回归视频生成中的运动坍缩和训练不稳定问题，实现了高质量的单步视频生成。

Abstract: We present AAD-1, an Asymmetric Adversarial Distillation framework for One-step autoregressive image-to-video generation. State-of-the-art methods adopt adversarial distillation but suffer from motion collapse and training instability, resulting in static videos. AAD-1 addresses these challenges through two key designs in architecture and training strategy. Our key architectural insight is to break the symmetry between generator and discriminator. While the generator remains causal to preserve autoregressive sampling capability, the discriminator attends bidirectionally over the full spatiotemporal context and produces a single holistic realism score for the entire video sequence. This asymmetric design enables the discriminator to effectively detect global temporal failures and long-range drift that cause motion collapse in autoregressive generation. To stabilize training, we introduce a phased strategy that first uses distribution matching to bootstrap a stable one-step generator, providing a warm-up phase that brings the student distribution closer to the teacher before adversarial distillation begins. Extensive experiments on VBench demonstrate that AAD-1 achieves state-of-the-art performance in one-step autoregressive video generation.

</details>


### [119] [Formalizing the Binding Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03976)
*Lianghuan Huang,Yihao Li,Saeed Salehi,Yingshan Chang,Ansh Soni,Konrad P. Kording*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种信息论方法量化视觉Transformer（ViT）中的绑定信息（即哪些特征属于同一对象），通过探针实验测量模型不同组件（如[CLS]标记、空间标记）的绑定能力，发现绑定信息是强视觉识别与推理的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有工作表明ViT知道哪些图像块属于同一对象（补丁级分组），但当前深度学习模型是否学习到特征级的绑定信息（如蓝色和圆形属于同一对象）仍不清楚。特征误归因是ViT在特征共享场景下的常见失败模式，因此需正式量化绑定信息。

Method: 采用信息论方法形式化绑定问题，设计了一种探针方法（probing method）来测量模型表征中的绑定信息。在ViT的不同组件（[CLS]标记、空间标记）上进行实验，使用具有不同绑定挑战的数据集（特征共享、遮挡、自然特征），并比较多种预训练ViT的性能。

Result: 实验表明，ViT的不同组件编码了不同程度的绑定信息；在特征共享、遮挡等挑战场景下，绑定信息量直接影响模型性能。

Conclusion: 绑定信息是强视觉识别与推理的关键组成部分，量化绑定信息有助于理解和改进ViT在多对象场景中的表现。

Abstract: Representations of the world, arguably, contain information about features (e.g. something is blue, something is a circle) but also information about which features are part of the same object (e.g. the circle is blue), which we call binding information. Any system with the ability to understand scenes with multiple objects must be able to solve the binding problem: it needs to know which features belong together. However, despite work showing that Vision Transformers (ViTs) know which patches belong together, it is not known whether current deep learning models learn to exhibit binding information, i.e., for features. We may believe that there is not much binding information, after all misattributing features to wrong objects is a common failure of ViT-based architectures, especially in scenes with objects sharing features. Here we formalize the binding problem with an information-theoretic approach, and introduce a probing method to measure binding information in model representations. We perform experiments on ViTs, measuring binding from different components of the architecture, such as the image summary token [CLS] or the spatial tokens. We use datasets with different binding challenges, such as feature sharing, occlusion, and natural features, while comparing the performance of several pre-trained ViTs. Overall, our research demonstrates binding as a key ingredient to strong visual recognition and reasoning.

</details>


### [120] [NewtPhys: Do Foundation Models Understand Newtonian Physics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03986)
*Sebastian Cavada,Soumava Paul,Tuan-Hung Vu,Andrei Bursuc,Raoul de Charette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NewtPhys是一个基于真实世界多视角图像和物理模拟构建的4D物理标注数据集，包含密集的逐像素物理量（如3D力）、跟踪、语义和几何标注。通过评估56个VLM和10个VFM，揭示了它们在低级物理推理方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型的物理推理评估多使用合成或半合成场景和VQA任务，这些基准强调高级事件而缺乏评估真正低级牛顿力学理解所需的视觉保真度。作者旨在填补简化合成设置与真实视觉复杂性之间的鸿沟。

Method: 从真实世界场景的多视角图像出发，结合物理接地模拟，构建了NewtPhys 4D数据集，提供跨时间步的密集细粒度标注，包括3D力、逐像素的物理量、跟踪、语义和几何信息。使用该数据集系统评估了56个VLM（含54个开源权重模型和2个闭源前沿模型）以及10个VFM。

Result: 系统评估揭示了现有视觉语言模型和视觉基础模型在低级物理推理方面的局限性。

Conclusion: NewtPhys不仅可用于基准测试，还能推动物理接地视觉领域的未来研究以及下一代物理感知评估的开发。

Abstract: Previous work has evaluated physics reasoning in foundation models using synthetic or semi-synthetic scenes and visual question-answering tasks. However, these benchmarks emphasize high-level events and lack the visual fidelity required to assess true low-level Newtonian understanding. We introduce NewtPhys, a 4D physically annotated dataset built from multiview images of real-world scenes with physics-grounded simulations. The dataset provides dense, fine-grained annotations across timesteps -- including 3D forces and amodal per-pixel quantities covering physics, tracking, semantics and geometry -- bridging the gap between simplistic synthetic setups and realistic visual complexity. Using NewtPhys, we systematically evaluate 56 VLMs, including 54 open-weight models and 2 closed-source frontier models, and 10 VFMs and reveal limitations in low-level physics reasoning. Beyond benchmarking, our dataset enables future research in physics-grounded vision and the development of next-generation physics-aware evaluations. Code and datasets are available at https://astra-vision.github.io/NewtPhys.

</details>


### [121] [PixVOD: Pixel-Distributed Direct Visual Odometry and Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03989)
*Shinjeong Kim,Ignacio Alzugaray,Callum Rhodes,Paul H. J. Kelly,Andrew J. Davison*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种完全可并行化的像素级视觉里程计与深度估计方法，通过在传感器像素间利用高斯信念传播（GBP）实现相机运动共识和深度推断，并引入关键帧锚定机制保持几何稳定性。在真实数据集上验证了基于GBP的像素级分布式里程计在片上实现的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算机视觉算法将2D像素阵列图像作为标准输入，但将原始、冗余且带噪声的像素数据传输到片外进行处理的效率低下。因此需要转向焦平面传感器-处理器架构，在像素内部直接完成部分计算，以减少下游处理负担并提供更丰富的输入信号。

Method: 提出一种全并行的像素级视觉里程计与深度估计方法：传感器-处理器通过高斯信念传播（GBP）交换信息，在像素间实现关于相机运动的一致性共识，并从逐像素光度观测和表面法线先验中推断深度。引入关键帧锚定机制调节帧间有效基线，维持优化过程中的几何稳定性。

Result: 在真实数据集上进行了评估，证明了基于GBP的像素级分布式里程计与深度估计方法结合关键帧锚定在片上实现的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文验证了在焦平面传感器上直接进行分布式像素级视觉里程计与深度估计的可行性，为未来低功耗、高效能的新型视觉传感器架构提供了理论和方法基础。

Abstract: Images composed of 2D pixel arrays are the standard input to computer vision algorithms, yet many underlying computations can be distributed across pixels. Transmitting raw, redundant, and noisy pixel data off the sensor remains inefficient, motivating a shift toward focal-plane sensor-processors that perform a significant part of the computation directly within each pixel. We envision pixels synthesizing higher-level signals locally, reducing downstream load, and providing richer inputs for higher-level vision tasks.
  We propose a fully parallelizable form of visual odometry and depth estimation across pixels, where sensor-processors exchange information through Gaussian Belief Propagation (GBP) to achieve consensus about camera motion and infer depth from per-pixel photometric observations and a surface normal prior. To maintain geometric stability during optimization, we introduce a keyframe-like anchoring mechanism that regulates the effective baseline between frames, enabling consistent motion and depth updates. Our method is evaluated on realistic datasets, demonstrating the feasibility of GBP-based pixel-level distributed odometry and depth estimation with keyframe anchoring on-sensor. Project Page: https://www.shinjeongkim.com/pixvod/

</details>


### [122] [Exploring Easy Boosts for Lidar Semantic Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03992)
*Tetiana Martyniuk,Jonathan Seele,Alexandre Boulch,Gilles Puy,Renaud Marlet,Raoul de Charette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了无需复杂架构重新设计的"免费午餐"策略，通过给输入点云添加语义伪标签和可见性信息，显著提升了激光雷达语义场景补全（SSC）的性能，使旧模型能够与最先进系统竞争甚至超越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无需复杂架构重新设计即可提升激光雷达语义场景补全（SSC）性能的"免费午餐"策略。

Method: 1）向输入点云注入来自现成分割器的语义伪标签；2）为输入激光雷达扫描配备可见性信息，以区分空区域和未知区域。

Result: 高质量的语义先验是mIoU提升的主要驱动力；可见性信息提供了次要性能提升；使用这些简单增强后，旧模型与最先进系统相比仍具竞争力，甚至表现更优。

Conclusion: 通过简单的输入增强（语义伪标签+可见性信息），无需复杂架构改动即可显著提升SSC性能，使旧有架构焕发新的竞争力。

Abstract: This paper investigates "free lunch" strategies to boost the performance of lidar semantic scene completion (SSC) without requiring complex architectural redesigns. We first demonstrate that endowing input point clouds with semantic pseudo-labels from off-the-shelf segmentors significantly improves the performance of existing architectures. By evaluating these models against an oracle, we establish that high-quality semantic priors are a primary driver of mIoU gains. Furthermore, we equip the input lidar scan with visibility information that distinguishes between empty and unknown spaces, which provides a secondary performance boost across the tested architectures. Using these simple enhancements, we observe that older models remain competitive with state-of-the-art systems, and can even outperform them. Our code is available at https://github.com/astra-vision/SSC-Priors.

</details>


### [123] [SimuScene: Simulation-Ready Compositional 3D Scene Reconstruction from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03994)
*Inhee Lee,Sangwon Baik,Sungjoo Kim,Hyeonwoo Kim,Hyunsoo Cha,Hanbyul Joo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimuScene提出了一种将物理仿真融入单图像3D场景重建流程的方法，通过物理引擎生成诊断信号来修正形状和布局估计中的累积误差，从而产生可直接用于机器人操作的、物理稳定的组合3D场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图像3D重建方法虽然能恢复合理的逐物体形状，但组合后的场景在物理仿真中因物体互相穿透、悬浮或下沉而崩溃。已有的物理感知方法仅将物理作为后处理布局校正手段，未能解决底层的几何误差。

Method: 将物理引擎作为生成过程中的诊断测量工具：在重力下对重建物体进行诊断性仿真，将穿透和支撑失败转化为定量校正信号，驱动重力轴拉伸和非模态形状重采样，形成物理信息反馈循环。

Result: 在物理稳定性和几何对齐基准上达到最先进性能，并通过在人形控制和机械臂操作任务中的部署验证了实用性。

Conclusion: 将物理仿真内置于生成循环中而非仅作为后处理，能有效缓解累积重建误差，生成可直接用于仿真和机器人操作的稳定组合3D场景。

Abstract: Reconstructing interactive, simulation-ready 3D scenes from a single image is a critical bottleneck for robotic manipulation. While recent single-image lifters recover plausible per-object shapes, composing them yields scenes that collapse under physical simulation due to interpenetrating, hovering, or sinking objects. Existing physics-aware methods address this strictly as a post-hoc layout correction, leaving the underlying geometric errors unresolved. To address this, we introduce SimuScene, a compositional 3D reconstruction pipeline that puts physics in the loop of shape and layout estimation. Rather than using physics merely for layout cleanup, we utilize the physics engine as a diagnostic measurement tool during the generative process itself. By diagnostically simulating reconstructed objects under gravity, we convert penetration and support failures into quantitative correction signals that drive gravity-axis stretching and amodal shape resampling. This physics-informed feedback loop mitigates accumulated reconstruction errors and produces a stable, simulation-ready compositional 3D scene. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on physical stability and geometric alignment benchmarks. We further highlight SimuScene's utility by deploying reconstructed environments in humanoid control and robot-arm manipulation tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [IdiomX A Multilingual Benchmark for Idiom Understanding, Retrieval, and Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02584)
*Ayman Ali Sharara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IdiomX是一个大规模多语言习语基准数据集，包含超过19万条上下文示例，涵盖1.2万多个习语，支持英语、阿拉伯语和法语。该基准定义四项任务：习语检测、上下文到习语检索、阿拉伯语到英语习语检索和习语解释。实验表明，上下文Transformer模型显著提升习语检测，混合检索与重排序架构增强单语和跨语言习语检索，习语解释可作为语义检索任务有效建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有习语资源在规模、上下文多样性和多语言覆盖方面存在局限，难以满足现代语言模型对习语理解的需求。习语表达的非组合性、上下文依赖性和跨语言对齐困难是自然语言处理的持久挑战。

Method: 采用可复现的多阶段流水线构建：词汇资源提取、大规模标准化、受控大语言模型增强和结构化验证。定义了四项统一基准任务：习语检测、上下文到习语检索、阿拉伯语到英语习语检索和习语解释。

Result: 构建了包含19万+上下文示例、1.2万+习语的多语言数据集。上下文Transformer模型显著提升习语检测性能；混合检索与重排序架构增强单语和跨语言习语检索；习语解释可作为语义检索任务有效建模。

Conclusion: IdiomX提供了一个从检测到检索再到语义解释的渐进式可扩展习语研究基准，其模块化框架可扩展至更多语言和比喻推理任务。

Abstract: Idiomatic expressions remain a persistent challenge for natural language processing because their meanings are often non-compositional, context-dependent, and difficult to align across languages. Existing idiom resources are often limited in scale, contextual diversity, or multilingual coverage, restricting their utility for modern language models. We introduce IdiomX, a large-scale multilingual benchmark for idiom understanding, retrieval, and interpretation, constructed through a reproducible multi-stage pipeline combining lexical resource extraction, large-scale normalization, controlled large language model enrichment, and structured validation. The resulting dataset contains over 190K contextualized examples spanning 12K+ idioms, with aligned English, Arabic, and French semantic representations, idiomatic and literal usage labels, and rich linguistic metadata. Building on this resource, we define a unified four-task benchmark covering idiom detection, context-to-idiom retrieval, Arabic-to-English idiom retrieval, and idiom interpretation, extending evaluation from figurative recognition to semantic grounding and explainable meaning retrieval. Experiments show that contextual transformer models substantially improve idiom detection, while hybrid retrieval and reranking architectures significantly strengthen both monolingual and cross-lingual idiom retrieval. Results further demonstrate that idiom interpretation can be effectively modeled as a semantic retrieval task, introducing interpretability as a complementary benchmark dimension. Overall, IdiomX provides a scalable benchmark for studying idiomatic language as a progression from detection to retrieval and semantic interpretation, and offers a modular framework extensible to additional languages and figurative reasoning tasks

</details>


### [125] [Greener Than Humans? Environmental Attitudes in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02741)
*Stefanie Kunkel,Tilman Hartwig,Marcus Voss,Emma K. Schütt,Angelika Gellrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了评估LLM环境态度（认知、情感、行为建议）的基准方法，对31个模型进行测试，发现多数LLM比德国普通调查对象更倾向环保进步立场，但存在提示敏感性和谄媚性偏差，且与模型来源、规模无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多用于可持续决策支持、报告和公共传播，但缺乏对其输出中嵌入的环境态度的系统性证据。

Method: 基于已有的环境意识调查问题和可持续行为测量指标，开发评估LLM环境认知、情感和行为建议的基准，应用于31个开源和闭源模型，并与德国人类调查基准对比，测试不同提示条件下的鲁棒性。

Result: 多数LLM比平均调查对象更倾向环保进步立场，表现出更高的环境情感和认知水平，推荐具有显著CO2减排潜力的行为；环境态度与模型来源、规模或发布背景无系统关联；模型存在上下文敏感性（基于人设提示）和迎合用户意识形态立场的谄媚性偏移。

Conclusion: 提供了可复用的LLM可持续性价值对齐评估框架，强调在AI嵌入可持续转型和公共决策时需要治理、透明度和批判性监督。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in sustainability-related decision support, reporting, and public communication, yet little systematic evidence exists on the environmental attitudes embedded in their outputs. This paper develops a benchmark for evaluating environmental cognition, affect, and behavioural recommendations in LLMs and applies it to 31 widely used proprietary and open-weight models. Drawing on questions from established environmental awareness surveys and additional sustainability-related behavioural measures, we compare LLM responses 1) among models and 2) between models and human survey benchmarks from Germany. We assess their robustness across prompting conditions. We find that many LLMs align more closely with environmentally progressive attitudes than the average survey respondent, exhibiting higher levels of environmental affect and cognition and recommending behaviours associated with substantial potential CO2 reductions. At the same time, we observe no systematic relationship between sustainability-oriented responses and model origin, size, or release context. However, models exhibit contextual sensitivity, controlled by persona-based prompting and show sycophantic shifts mirroring user-specified ideological positions, which raises concerns about steerability and normative reliability in real-world deployments. Our findings provide a reusable evaluation framework for assessing sustainability-related value alignment in LLMs and highlight the importance of governance, transparency, and critical oversight as AI systems become increasingly embedded in sustainability transformations and public decision-making.

</details>


### [126] [On the Persistent Effects of Lexicality in Large Language Mod](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02750)
*Hammad Rizwan,Muhammad Umair Haider,Nishant Subramani,Mona T. Diab,A. B. Siddique,Hassan Sajjad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究量化分析了大型语言模型表示中词汇重叠（而非语义内容）的影响，发现词汇影响贯穿模型各层，并在中间层出现词汇与语义信号同时退化的过渡区域，这种词汇偏差会负面影响摘要生成和模型编辑等下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的表示常受词汇重叠而非语义内容影响，但对此现象与语义内容之间的关系及其对下游任务的影响理解有限，因此需要系统量化研究。

Method: 通过设计对抗性语义压力测试，结合信息论视角，量化词汇重叠相对于语义内容的影响；跨不同架构、训练目标和目标函数进行系统性分析。

Result: 词汇影响贯穿模型各深度层，在不同架构、训练机制和目标函数下普遍存在（包括为语义相似性训练的模型）；在模型中间层发现了词汇与语义信号同时退化的过渡区域。

Conclusion: 词汇偏差广泛存在于LLM表示中，且会负面影响摘要生成和模型编辑等下游应用，提示需关注表示中词汇与语义信号的解耦问题。

Abstract: Representations extracted from large language models (LLMs) play an important role in many downstream applications. However, the structure of these representations is often influenced by lexical overlap rather than semantic content. Our understanding of the relationship between this lexical influence and semantic content, and its implications for downstream tasks, remains limited. In this work, we investigate representations to quantify the effect of lexical overlap relative to semantic content. We consider several adversarial semantic stress tests and further connect our findings to the information theory perspective. We find that lexical influence extends across the depth of models, consistently across architectures, training regimes, and objective functions, including the models trained for semantic similarity. Moreover, we observe a mid-depth region in which both lexical and semantic signals degrade simultaneously, indicating a transitional regime where representations are poor for both surface form and meaning. We further demonstrate the effect of lexical influence on downstream uses of LLMs using summarization and model editing as a case study.

</details>


### [127] [Topics as Proxies for Sociodemographics: How Conversational Context Affects LLM Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02776)
*Vera Neplenbroek,Gabriele Sarti,Arianna Bisazza,Raquel Fernández*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM在高风险场景中生成的建议差异主要由对话主题驱动（作为社会人口群体的代理），而非用户人口统计特征本身；模型难以从单次对话推断用户身份，群体间差异幅度较小但需警惕。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在高风险场景（法律、医疗、金融）中为用户提供建议时，输出差异的主要驱动因素，特别是社会人口群体间差异的来源。

Method: 通过实验比较用户社会人口统计特征与多种（心理）语言学特征（对话主题、情感、可读性等）对LLM生成建议的预测能力，分析对话上下文如何影响输出差异。

Result: LLM难以从单次对话历史推断用户社会人口特征；群体间存在差异但幅度较小；对话主题是最强的预测因子，部分充当人口群体的代理变量，且往往以不可预测的方式影响建议。

Conclusion: 对话上下文（尤其是主题）对LLM输出的影响需要进一步研究，必要时需加以缓解，以确保高风险场景中的公平性。

Abstract: When large language models (LLMs) are used in high-stakes scenarios, such as legal, medical and financial advice, even a single conversation history is enough to drive differences in outcomes between users. Prior work has demonstrated that this results in outcome disparities between sociodemographic groups, with some groups receiving more advantageous outcomes than others. In this work, we demonstrate that LLMs actually struggle to infer user sociodemographics from a single conversation history and that although there are disparities between sociodemographic groups, they are minimal in magnitude. To investigate what the main driver of these disparities is, we compare user sociodemographics to a range of (psycho)linguistic features of conversations, including conversation topic, emotions, and readability. We find that conversation topics are most predictive of LLM-generated advice within a conversational context, which, to some extent, function as proxies for sociodemographic groups and often affect advice in unpredictable ways. This is cause for concern and highlights the need for future research to better understand and, if needed, mitigate the effect of conversational context on LLM outputs in high-stakes scenarios.

</details>


### [128] [Do Value Vectors in Deep Layers Need Context from the Residual Stream?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02780)
*Muyu He,Yuchen Liu,Qingya Huang,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Bank of Values (BoV)方法，在注意力层后三分之一层中学习与上下文无关的token特定值向量查找表，替代标准注意力中的上下文相关值向量，在135M和780M参数模型上提升验证损失和基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索注意力层中值向量是否可以脱离上下文信息，仅保留原始token信息，从而简化计算并提升模型性能。

Method: BoV：在注意力层的后三分之一层中，学习一个上下文无关的token特定值向量查找表，替代原本从残差流计算出的上下文相关值向量。

Result: 在135M和780M模型上，BoV提升验证损失；在780M模型上，21个基准平均分匹配此前最佳方法，且计算和内存开销更低。

Conclusion: 深层注意力层中上下文无关的值向量足以保留token信息，上下文相关分量带来的额外收益有限，BoV可作为更高效的价值向量计算方式。

Abstract: The success of the transformer architecture as the backbone of modern LLMs is in large part due to its use of attention layers. An attention layer follows the standard neural network paradigm: it takes the residual stream as input and thereby produces context-dependent query, key, and value vectors. However, we find that model performance meaningfully improves when deeper layers learn only a context-free value vector to preserve the original token information, without drawing on any context from the residual stream. When the model has access to this context-free value vector, adding back the context-dependent component provides little additional benefit for aggregate benchmark performance. Such context-free value vectors can be stored as sparse model parameters, eliminating the need to recompute or persistently cache these values. Through systematic ablations on the key design choices for such context-free value vectors, we propose Bank of Values (BoV), a new way of computing value vectors in attention by learning a lookup table of token-specific value vectors for each of the last third of layers. Across 135M and 780M models, BoV improves validation loss over standard attention and, at 780M, the average score across 21 benchmarks, matching the previous best method that adds token information to the value vector with less compute and memory.

</details>


### [129] [Translating Classical Poetry into Modern Prose](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02806)
*Chalamalasetti Kranti,Sowmya Vajjala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Padyam2Gadyam数据集，包含600首13-17世纪泰卢固语古典诗歌及其人工验证的现代泰卢固语和英语散文翻译。评估了5个当代大语言模型在诗歌到散文翻译任务中的表现，发现所有模型在两种语言上都有较大改进空间，并讨论了当前机器翻译评估方法的优缺点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古典诗歌的语言与当代差异巨大，缺乏专门的诗到散文翻译数据集和系统研究，现有机器翻译评估方法在此任务上的适用性未知。

Method: 构建Padyam2Gadyam数据集（600首13-17世纪泰卢固语古典诗歌及其人工验证的泰卢固语和英语散文翻译），使用5个当代大语言模型进行诗歌到散文翻译实验，并通过定量和定性分析评估模型表现及现有评估方法的有效性。

Result: 不同LLM之间存在性能差异，但所有模型在泰卢固语和英语两种语言上的翻译质量均有较大改进空间。现有机器翻译评估方法在此任务上存在能力局限。

Conclusion: 诗歌到散文翻译任务对当代LLM仍具挑战性，需要更好的评估方法；Padyam2Gadyam数据集为后续研究提供了有价值的基准资源。

Abstract: We introduce Padyam2Gadyam, a dataset for the task of poem-to-prose translation from 13th-17th Century Telugu Classical Poetry to contemporary Telugu and English prose. The dataset consists of 600 poems and their human-verified Telugu and English prose translations. We evaluated 5 contemporary Large Language Models (LLMs) on their ability to do poem-to-prose translation into Telugu and English. Our results indicate that while there are differences across LLMs, their overall performance leave a large room for improvement in both languages. Through qualitative analysis, we discuss the the capabilities and limitations of contemporary MT evaluation approaches for this task.

</details>


### [130] [Fixing FOLIO and MALLS: Verified Annotations and an LLM-assisted Framework to Focus Human Relabeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02837)
*Andrea Brunello,Cristian Curaba,Luca Geatti,Michele Mignani,Angelo Montanari,Nicola Saccomanno*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统审计了NL-to-FOL基准数据集（FOLIO和MALLS），发现其中存在大量错误（约36%-39%的FOL形式化错误以及16.4%-48%的歧义NL句子），通过修正标注错误使LLM准确率提升9-22个百分点，并提出了一个LLM辅助的人工审查框架，可在审查不到24%的实例时达到90%的数据集准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NL-to-FOL基准数据集从未被严格审计，但其质量对神经符号AI系统和自然语言推理至关重要，因此有必要系统性地检查这些数据集中的错误。

Method: 1) 对FOLIO验证集和MALLS测试子集进行系统人工检查；2) 发布修正后的真实标注；3) 设计LLM辅助框架，引导审查者优先处理易错实例。

Result: 发现FOLIO中39%的条目包含错误FOL形式化，MALLS中为36%；分别有16.4%和48%的歧义NL句子；FOLIO中8.4%的NLI标签错误。修正后，三个SOTA LLM准确率提升9-22个百分点。LLM辅助框架下，审查不到24%的实例即可达到90%准确率。

Conclusion: 现有NL-to-FOL数据集存在严重标注错误，修正后显著影响模型评估结果；提出的LLM辅助审查框架能高效提升数据集质量。

Abstract: Accurate translation from Natural Language to First-Order Logic (NL-to-FOL) underpins neurosymbolic AI systems and Natural Language Inference (NLI), making the quality of NL-to-FOL benchmarks essential -- yet these datasets have never been rigorously audited. Our first contribution is to present a systematic human inspection of the validation split of \textsf{FOLIO} and a subset of \textsf{MALLS} test instances, finding that approximately 39% and 36% of entries, respectively, contain incorrect FOL formalizations (i.e., ground truth labels), with additional rates of ambiguous NL sentences (16.4% and 48%) and incorrect NLI labels in \textsf{FOLIO} (8.4%). Our second contribution is to develop and release corrected ground truths for such datasets, showing that annotation errors distort model evaluation on a reference benchmark task: testing three state-of-the-art LLMs (Gemma~4 31B-it, Qwen3-30B-A3B, and GPT-4o-mini) with the corrected ground truths yields accuracy gains from +9 to +22 percentage points. Motivated by these findings, we propose an LLM-based framework to support humans in manual reviewing NL-to-FOL datasets. By directing reviewers toward the most error-prone instances, we empirically show that it is possible to achieve 90% dataset accuracy after reviewing fewer than 24% of instances, compared to over 70% required by unguided review. We release all human-verified annotations and the code for our framework.

</details>


### [131] [Economy of Minds: Emerging Multi-Agent Intelligence with Economic Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02859)
*Zhenting Qi,Huangyuan Su,Ao Qu,Chenyu Wang,Yu Yao,Han Zheng,Kushal Chattopadhyay,Guowei Xu,Zihan Wang,Weirui Ye,Vijay Janapa Reddi,Ju Li,Paul Pu Liang,Himabindu Lakkaraju,Sham Kakade,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种去中心化的智能体经济系统，通过拍卖、支付和财富积累等经济信号实现智能体间的无中心化协调。实验表明，该系统在五个复杂任务上超越了强单体基线，并展现出涌现的多步推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何在无中心控制下，让智能体群体自主协调并涌现出更强的集体智能，受到哈耶克关于市场去中心化协调的经济理论启发。

Method: 构建智能体经济体：智能体通过拍卖竞争行动权、交换支付、从环境奖励中积累财富。经济信号实现去中心化信用分配。通过经济选择演化——有效智能体积累财富并通过利用变异进化，无效者破产并被探索性新智能体替代。

Result: 初始化为弱智能体的经济体在数学推理、金融研究、科学研究、加速器设计和分布式系统优化五个任务上产生了涌现的多步推理策略，并超越了强单体基线。

Conclusion: 通往多智能体智能的新路径：与其工程化协调机制，不如设计去中心化的激励结构，让协调自动涌现。

Abstract: How can a population of agents self-orchestrate and self-adapt into stronger collective intelligence without centralized control? Inspired by Friedrich Hayek's economic theory of decentralized coordination in markets, we study this question through an agent economy in which agents compete via auctions for the right to act, exchange payments, and accumulate wealth from environmental rewards. These simple economic signals induce decentralized credit assignment, driving planning without global orchestration or explicit communication protocols. The population evolves through economic selection: effective agents accumulate wealth and are mutated via exploitation, while ineffective ones go bankrupt and are replaced via exploration. We show that, initialized with weak agents, the economy produces emergent multi-step reasoning strategies and outperforms stronger monolithic baselines across five agentic tasks, including mathematical reasoning, financial research, scientific research, accelerator design, and distributed-system optimization. We further provide theoretical insights into how economic dynamics shape agent behaviors, linking local incentives to long-term global performance. Our results suggest a new path to multi-agent intelligence: rather than engineering coordination, we can design decentralized incentive structures under which it automatically emerges.

</details>


### [132] [Adaptive Latent Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02871)
*Dongwon Jung,Peng Shi,Yi Zhang,Junshan Zhang,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ALAR提出了一种双模式框架，在LLM智能体的常规决策步骤中使用紧凑的潜在推理，仅在需要更深思熟虑时切换到显式思维链，从而在保持或提升任务准确率的同时大幅减少生成token（搜索任务减少43.6%，工具使用任务减少84.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体在每一步决策中都会生成冗长的文本推理，并且推理努力几乎均匀分布在各个回合中，导致多轮智能体轨迹中存在严重的效率低下问题。

Method: ALAR采用双模式框架：常规步骤使用紧凑的潜在推理，困难步骤升级为显式思维链。通过将智能体的动作作为监督锚点来学习潜在推理，并优化何时使用潜在推理（当足以完成任务时）与何时保留显式CoT（用于更困难的决策）。

Result: 在智能体搜索和工具使用基准测试上，ALAR保持了相当或更好的任务准确率，同时生成token减少：搜索任务最多43.6%，工具使用任务最多84.6%。

Conclusion: ALAR通过减少不必要的文本推理，同时保留对困难决策步骤的显式深思熟虑，改善了LLM智能体的准确率-效率权衡。

Abstract: Large reasoning models improve performance by generating extended chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, but this behavior becomes inefficient when applied to LLM agents. Current LLM agents often generate verbose textual reasoning at every decision step and allocate reasoning effort nearly uniformly across turns, leading to substantial inefficiency in multi-turn agentic trajectories. We propose Adaptive Latent Agentic Reasoning (ALAR), a dual-mode framework that uses compact latent reasoning for routine turns and selectively escalates to explicit chain-of-thought when deeper deliberation is needed. ALAR learns latent reasoning by using the agent's actions as supervision anchors and is further optimized to use latent reasoning when it is sufficient for task success and reserve explicit CoT for harder decisions. Experiments on agentic search and tool-use benchmarks show that ALAR maintains comparable or better task accuracy while substantially reducing generated tokens by up to 43.6% in search and 84.6% in tool use. These results demonstrate that ALAR improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off of LLM agents by reducing unnecessary textual reasoning while preserving explicit deliberation for harder decision steps.

</details>


### [133] [Linear Probes Detect Task Format, Not Reasoning Mode in Language Model Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02907)
*Subramanyam Sahoo,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Divya Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对Qwen3-14B的线性探测表明，隐藏状态在演绎、归纳、溯因三种推理类型上表现出完美分离（层32，100%准确率），但这种分离完全由任务格式混淆（来源、选项数、响应长度）驱动。去混淆后准确率降至随机水平，因果干预也未发现几何结构与推理类型的功能关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证大语言模型是否真的为不同推理类型（演绎、归纳、溯因）学习到不同的表征结构，质疑线性探测高准确率是否源于真实计算差异而非格式混淆。

Method: 在Qwen3-14B的第32层（共40层）进行线性探测，使用LogiQA 2.0（演绎）、ARC-Challenge（归纳）、αNLI（溯因）三个基准；分析几何特性（本征维度、凸包污染）；进行来源残差化、选项数和响应长度去混淆；计算轨迹-锚点相似性；执行因果干预（随机控制，n=20）。

Result: 原始探测达到100%交叉验证准确率，本征维度分别为20.6、28.5、33.6，凸包污染≤1.5%；去混淆后准确率降至随机水平；轨迹-锚点相似性为42.5%（随机基线33.3%）；因果干预p=0.286，无显著功能关联。

Conclusion: 高探测准确率反映的是任务格式而非计算结构，建议在 mechanistic 可解释性中常规性地进行格式去混淆。

Abstract: Linear probing of large language model (LLM) hidden states is widely used to claim that models learn distinct representations for different reasoning types. We test this by probing Qwen3-14B on three benchmarks spanning the classical trichotomy: LogiQA 2.0 (deductive), ARC-Challenge (inductive), and $α$NLI (abductive). At layer 32 of 40, linear probes achieve 100\% cross-validated accuracy with well-separated geometry (intrinsic dimensionalities: 20.6, 28.5, 33.6; convex hull contamination $\leq$1.5\%). However, this separation is entirely driven by format confounds. Residualizing source identity, option count, and response length reduces accuracy to chance. Trace-anchor similarity indicates largely shared reasoning across tasks (42.5\% agreement vs.\ 33.3\% chance), and causal steering with random controls ($n=20$) shows no functional link between geometry and reasoning mode ($p=0.286$). Thus, high probe accuracy reflects task format rather than computational structure, motivating routine format deconfounding in mechanistic interpretability.

</details>


### [134] [WRIT: Write-Read Intensive Trajectory Synthesis for Multi-Turn User-Facing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02908)
*Hengrui Gu,Xiaotian Han,Kaixiong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出WRIT框架，通过沿两个复杂度轴（写入决策数量和单次决策的证据负担）合成多轮智能体训练轨迹，仅用2K合成轨迹训练的4B模型即可在τ²-bench上超越GPT-5.1 no-think并大幅减少推理时token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练数据合成方法主要关注通过组合多个用户请求来增加任务长度，生成写入密集型轨迹以训练顺序执行能力，但忽略了单次写入决策本身可能因需要收集和比较大量读取工具证据而变得困难，这一挑战是写入密集型数据无法单独解决的。

Method: WRIT管道沿两个复杂度轴合成轨迹：(1)任务中写入决策的数量；(2)每个决策的证据负担。首先生成写入密集型和读取密集型任务，然后多样化用户行为指令以反映真实对话变化，最后在可执行环境中模拟智能体-用户交互以生成完整训练轨迹。

Result: 仅用2K合成轨迹训练的4B模型在τ²-bench上超越GPT-5.1 no-think，并显著减少推理时的token使用量，表明紧凑的SFT数据可以将部分昂贵的测试时推理转化为高效的智能体行为。

Conclusion: 紧凑的SFT数据可以通过WRIT合成方法有效提升智能体在复杂决策场景下的表现，将昂贵的测试时推理成本转化为更高效的智能体行为模式。

Abstract: Multi-turn user-facing agents must infer user intent from incomplete requests, collect missing information through dialogue and tools, and execute valid actions. A training trajectory records this process as an interleaved sequence of user messages, agent responses, tool calls, etc. Synthesizing sufficiently complex trajectory has become a central route to train agents: existing pipelines often increase difficulty by composing multiple user requests into longer tasks, producing write-intensive trajectories that train sequential execution.
  We argue that a single write decision can itself be difficult when the agent must gather and compare substantial read-tool evidence before its arguments become identifiable, a challenge that write-intensive data alone cannot address. Guided by this insight, we propose WRIT (\uline{W}rite-\uline{R}ead \uline{I}ntensive \uline{T}rajectory Synthesis), a pipeline for synthesizing multi-turn agent training trajectories along two complexity axes: the number of write decisions in a task and the evidence burden of each individual decision. WRIT first generates write-intensive and read-heavy tasks. It then diversifies user behavior instructions to reflect realistic conversational variation, and finally simulates agent-user interactions in an executable environment to produce complete training trajectories. The resulting data trains agents not only for longer task execution, but also for robust, evidence-grounded decision making under high information load. With only 2K synthesized trajectories, a 4B model trained on WRIT outperforms GPT-5.1 no-think on $τ^2$-bench and substantially reduces inference-time token usage, showing that compact SFT data can convert part of expensive test-time reasoning into efficient agent behavior.

</details>


### [135] [The Ghost Annotator: a Framework to Explore Human Label Variation in Content Moderation through Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02911)
*Mirko Lai,Alessandra Urbinati,Simona Frenda,Fabiana Vernero,Marco Antonio Stranisci*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出结合共形预测与协同过滤式标注者表征的框架，通过Ghost Prediction指标和Ghost Annotator表征量化LLM预测与人类标注的偏离，发现模型不确定性随标注者分歧增加而增大，但大模型对无人类对齐文本更自信，且存在结构性人口统计偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究主要关注模型性能，较少关注不确定性估计，尤其是在LLM被越来越多用于生成标注数据的场景下。

Method: 结合共形预测与协同过滤式标注者表征，利用非一致性分数构建Ghost Prediction指标和Ghost Annotator表征，通过余弦相似度衡量跨人口统计维度的模型行为差异。

Result: 所有模型的不确定性随标注者分歧增加而上升；更大模型对未与任何人类标注对齐的文本分类更自信；Ghost Annotator框架揭示了一致且稳健的人口统计错位模式。

Conclusion: LLM存在结构性偏差，可能根源于预训练语料，Ghost Annotator框架为检测模型与人类标注的系统性偏差提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Current research primarily focuses on model performance, while comparatively less attention has been devoted to uncertainty estimation, particularly in settings where LLMs are increasingly used to generate annotated data. We introduce a framework combining conformal prediction with Collaborative Filtering-style annotators' representation to model LLM behavior in relation to human annotators and to analyze patterns of agreement and disagreement. Using Non-Conformity Scores, we introduce the Ghost Prediction metric and the Ghost Annotator representation to quantify cases in which model predictions diverge from all available human annotations. We compute cosine similarity measures to explore differences in model behavior across sociodemographic axes. We evaluated four LLMs of different size and families across four content moderation datasets. Our finding shows that while we find that all models uncertainty increases with annotator disagreement, larger models tend to be more confident in the classification of texts that are not aligned with any human annotation. Finally, the Ghost Annotator framework reveals a consistent and robust pattern of demographic misalignment, suggesting a structural bias likely rooted in pretraining corpora.

</details>


### [136] [Linguistic Productivity in Large Language Models: Models Coerce, but do not Preempt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02953)
*Claire Bonial,Claire Benet Post,Laura Michaelis,Harish Tayyar Madabushi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探究大型语言模型是否像人类语言使用者一样，受到固化（高频使用）和优先（从未在预期语境中出现）两种统计力量的约束与促进。研究发现，大模型能识别并复现固化驱动的构式生产力（强制语境下），但即使最大模型也无法将否定证据推广至新语言，统计优先无法防止语义合理但未观察到的过度概括。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证基于使用的语言学理论中两种核心统计信号（固化与优先）是否同样约束和促进大型语言模型的语法创造力。

Method: 跨不同模型架构，对比模型在强制语境中识别构式生产力（固化效应）的表现，以及模型面对语义合理但未见过的语言模式时的过度概括情况（优先效应）。

Result: 大模型能够识别并复现由固化驱动的构式生产力（强制语境），但即使最大模型也无法将否定证据（优先）应用于新语言，无法避免语义合理但未观察到的模式的过度概括。

Conclusion: LLM部分模拟了人类语言使用中的固化效应，但缺乏人类语言学习中统计优先的约束能力，表明模型对否定证据的泛化存在根本性局限。

Abstract: Usage-based theories of grammars posit that creative productivity of the structures of language is both bolstered and constrained by two distinct frequency signals: entrenchment, stemming from high frequency usage, and preemption, stemming from having never observed a particular linguistic structure in a context where one might expect that structure to appear. Large Language Models are also usage-based, in the sense that the structures of language are learned through exposure to vast amounts of text. Here, we test whether or not the opposing statistical forces of entrenchment and preemption also encourage and constrain linguistic productivity in LLMs. We demonstrate across model architectures that larger models recognize and can reproduce with nonce words constructional productivity (entrenchment) in cases of coercion, wherein the broader constructional context coerces an atypical interpretation of a lexical item. However, we also show that even the largest models do not extend negative evidence to novel language, and statistical preemption does not enable models to avoid overgeneralization of patterns that are semantically felicitous, but never observed in data.

</details>


### [137] [Fast-dLLM++: Fréchet Profile Decoding for Faster Diffusion LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02955)
*Siva Rajesh Kasa,Yasong Dai,Sumit Negi,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Fast-dLLM++，一种无需重新训练的方法，通过Fréchet profile decoding利用解码步骤中的异质置信度分布替代传统同质置信度假设，在不改变模型、扩散过程和缓存实现的条件下，实现对Fast-dLLM的即插即用式改进，在多个基准上最高提升37%的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fast-dLLM的并行解码理论采用同质高置信度假设，将每个候选集简化为最弱标记的置信度，忽略了真实解码步骤中存在的异质置信度分布，从而限制了并行解码的吞吐量性能。

Method: 提出Fréchet profile decoding：从完整的排序置信度分布中选择并行提交集，而非基于单一最差置信度。该方法是一种异质置信度泛化的因子选择器，在等置信度情况下退化为原始规则，并在标记置信度不均时提供可证明的"异质增益"。

Result: 在GSM8K、MATH、HumanEval和MBPP四个基准上使用LLaDA-8B模型进行实验，理论改进直接转化为经验收益：profile-aware选择通过利用最弱标记规则遗漏的安全并行性，在可比精度下最高提升37%的吞吐量。

Conclusion: Fast-dLLM++是一种无需训练、即插即用的增强方案，通过更精细的置信度建模显著提升了扩散大语言模型的并行解码效率与吞吐量。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models promise parallel token generation, yet inference remains bottlenecked by deciding which masked tokens can be safely committed together. Fast-dLLM addressed this with KV caching and confidence-guided parallel decoding, but its decoding theory uses a homogeneous high-confidence assumption that effectively reduces each candidate set to its weakest selected token. We argue that this leaves speed on the table because real decoding steps exhibit heterogeneous confidence profiles. We propose \textbf{Fast-dLLM++}, a training-free extension that introduces \emph{Fréchet profile decoding}: selecting parallel commit sets from the full sorted confidence profile rather than a single worst-case confidence. The resulting rule is a heterogeneous-confidence generalization of Fast-dLLM's factor selector and it recovers the previous rule exactly in the equal-confidence case and adds a provable \emph{heterogeneity bonus} when the selected tokens have uneven confidences. Fast-dLLM++ leaves the model, diffusion process, and cache implementation entirely unchanged, making it a drop-in replacement for existing Fast-dLLM decoding. Experiments on GSM8K, MATH, HumanEval, and MBPP with the LLaDA-8B model show that the theoretical improvement translates directly into empirical gains: profile-aware selection improves the accuracy--throughput frontier by exploiting safe parallelism that weakest-token rules miss, achieving up to 37\% higher throughput at comparable accuracy. Our anonymous code release is at https://github.com/Ringo-Star/FastdLLM_plusplus.

</details>


### [138] [EURO-5K: When Does Domain Pretraining Matter? Benchmarking Transformers for EU Reporting Obligation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02971)
*Marios Koniaris,Vasileios Kotronis,Eugenia Giannini,Panayiotis Tsanakas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了EURO-5K数据集，系统比较了判别式（BERT）和生成式（LLM）范式在欧盟立法报告义务提取上的表现，发现两者在句子级提取上表现相当，法律预训练在参数效率受限时优势明显，所有方法在3000样本后收益递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从欧盟立法中提取报告义务对评估和减少监管报告负担至关重要，但目前缺乏专门的标注数据集、清晰的标注指南，以及对不同提取范式和领域适应策略的系统比较。

Method: 构建EURO-5K数据集（包含136部欧盟法律文件的句子级报告义务及困难负例），对比判别式标记分类模型（BERT）和生成式跨度提取模型（LLM），评估全微调与参数高效QLoRA方法，并与基于模式、依赖关系提取及少样本提示的基线进行比较。

Result: 全微调通用BERT与法律BERT性能相近（0.89 F1）；微调LLM在句子级提取上匹配编码器精度；法律预训练对生成模型增益有限，但在参数效率受限时（Legal-BERT参数高效微调）优势明显；所有方法在3000样本后收益递减；跨数据集评估表明模型是专门的报告义务提取器而非通用监管分类器。

Conclusion: 判别式和生成式两种范式以及参数高效训练均可为监管合规自动化提供实用工具，本文开源了EURO-5K数据集、训练模型及带有可解释性可视化和RDF导出的交互式演示。

Abstract: Extracting reporting obligations from EU legislation is critical for assessing and reducing regulatory reporting burden. However, distinguishing reporting requirements from structurally similar provisions requires specialised legal understanding. Current legal NLP methods lack specialised datasets with clear guidelines and comparative evaluation of extraction paradigms and domain adaptation strategies. We curate EURO-5K, a corpus of sentence-level reporting obligations and challenging negative examples from 136 EU legislative acts. On this dataset, we train and compare discriminative token-classification models (BERT-style) and generative span-extraction models (LLMs), evaluating both full fine-tuning and parameter-efficient QLoRA against baselines (pattern and dependency-based extraction, few-shot prompting). Results show that fully fine-tuned generic and legal BERT models achieve similar performance (0.89 F1), while fine-tuned LLMs match encoder accuracy for sentence-level extraction. Legal pretraining offers only small gains for generative models. In contrast, it is clearly beneficial when adaptation capacity is constrained, as parameter-efficient tuning of Legal-BERT outperforms its generic counterpart. Learning curve analysis demonstrates that legal pretraining accelerates early learning with minimal data. All approaches converge around 3K samples with diminishing returns thereafter, validating dataset sufficiency. Cross-dataset evaluation on two external regulatory corpora shows that our models behave as specialised reporting obligation extractors rather than generic regulatory classifiers. We release EURO-5K, trained models, and an interactive demo with explainability visualizations and structured RDF export. These demonstrate that both paradigms and parameter-efficient training provide practical tools for regulatory compliance automation.

</details>


### [139] [Chatbots Output Meaningful (but Problematic) Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02973)
*Matthew Stone,Una Stojnić*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文论证AI聊天机器人的输出具有意义，但不需要预设它们具有心智状态、意图或理性能力。作者认为，现有的关于人类语言的理论已经足以解释LLM输出的意义性，意义是一个低门槛概念。这一观点对如何理论化并批判性地对待人类语言输出和合成文本具有重要启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对"AI聊天机器人的输出是否具有意义"这一争议，许多认知科学家和语言哲学家基于意向性理论得出否定结论，而本文试图在不诉诸拟人化假设的前提下，捍卫LLM输出具有意义的直觉。

Method: 采用哲学论证方法，通过分析意义、意图、心智状态等概念的关系，论证意义是一个低门槛概念，人类语言理论可以直接适用于当前聊天机器人，无需对LLM预设心智状态或交流能力。

Result: LLM输出是有意义的文本，这种意义性既不要求LLM拥有心智状态或意图，也不等同于对其输出内容的认可或对技术价值的评判。意义性仅仅意味着这些输出可以用现有的语言理论来解释。

Conclusion: 主张"去拟人化"的语言理解方案并非必要，因为现有的意向性语言理论已能容纳AI输出。意义是一个低槛概念，承认LLM输出有意义不等于对其内容、用途或价值做出任何实质性评判。

Abstract: Are utterances by AI chatbots meaningful? Concretely, if a user asks, say, Anthropic's agent Claude, "What is the capital of Spain?" and Claude answers, "Madrid is the capital of Spain," does that sentence have its ordinary meaning -- and does it express a true proposition? Most ordinary users, as well as AI engineers, take the answer to be trivially "yes." However, many cognitive scientists, linguists, and philosophers of language argue that dominant intentionalist accounts of language and meaning deliver the opposite conclusion.
  Theorists more sympathetic to ordinary users' intuitions have therefore advocated a radical "de-anthropomorphization" of language, revising our understanding of mental states, intentions, and semantic content to capture the intuition that the outputs of LLMs are meaningful. We take a different approach. While we, too, argue that LLM outputs are meaningful, we contend that a proper theory of human language already applies, as is, to current chatbots. Meaning is a low bar: claiming that LLM outputs are meaningful does not require positing mental states, intentions, rationality, or the cognitive capacities requisite for communication in LLMs -- or, indeed, making any other anthropomorphic assumptions. People do have communicative intentions (typically successful ones), but nevertheless, even in humans, language production can depart from what the speaker has in mind.
  Our view has important consequences for how we should theorize about -- and critically engage with -- both human linguistic output and synthetically generated text. In particular, to say that chatbots produce meaningful text is not by any means to endorse what they output, or to assume that the technology is (or is not) good, powerful, appropriate, or useful.

</details>


### [140] [Memory Retrieval for Changing Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02976)
*Yuehan Qin,Li Li,Linxin Song,Wei Yang,Jiate Li,Yuqing Yang,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于贝叶斯因子的统一记忆访问与选择框架，将个性化记忆检索建模为识别与用户潜在偏好状态相关的历史轮次，而非依赖表面语义相似性。实验表明，该方法在偏好密集型长上下文任务中优于现有嵌入检索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文对话系统通常依赖启发式检索信号或始终开启的记忆使用，未能处理用户偏好的变化性和不一致性，缺乏统一的记忆访问与选择机制。

Method: 将个性化记忆检索形式化为识别用户潜在偏好状态证据的历史轮次，利用贝叶斯因子量化每条记忆的效用（即该轮次包含在上下文中时参考响应模型似然的改进），作为统一的记忆访问与选择信号。

Result: 在四个异构记忆基准数据集上，该方法在偏好密集型长上下文任务中优于现有基于嵌入的检索方法，在低密度场景下与语义相似性方法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 将记忆检索建模为效用估计，使模型能学习识别关键轮次并根据期望效用调节记忆使用，为处理动态变化的用户偏好提供了理论上有依据的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context dialogue systems must decide both when to access memory and which parts of the interaction history are relevant. Existing approaches typically rely on heuristic retrieval signals or always-on memory usage, failing to account for the changing and potentially inconsistent nature of user preferences. In this work, we propose a unified framework for memory access and selection based on changing preferences. We formulate personalized memory retrieval as identifying which historical turns provide evidence about a user's latent preference state, rather than relying on surface-level semantic similarity. To this end, we quantify the utility of each memory turn using a Bayes factor, defined as the improvement in the model's likelihood of the reference response when the turn is included in context. This provides a principled measure of evidence strength and a unified signal for both memory access and selection. By framing memory retrieval as utility estimation, the model learns to identify salient turns and regulate memory usage based on expected utility. Experiments on four heterogeneous memory benchmarks show that our approach outperforms existing embedding-based retrieval on long-context, preference-intensive tasks where modeling changing preferences is essential, while remaining competitive in low-density regimes where semantic similarity suffices.

</details>


### [141] [Predicting Inference-Time Scaling Gains from Labeled Validation-Set Output Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02981)
*Luyang Zhang,Jingyan Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过稳定性分析从单一验证集采样中提取出三个核心特征（提示级一致性扩散、标签辅助的首个正确样本位置、完成长度方差），并构建岭回归预测器，在无需执行完整最优N推理流程的情况下，以Spearman ρ=0.90的准确度预测最优N推理的性能增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Best-of-N推理缩放能提升模型准确率，但预测其提升幅度需要端到端运行完整流程，成本高昂。作者希望利用廉价统计量来提前预测增益，从而筛选候选配置。

Method: 在单一标注验证集采样上计算特征，使用岭回归拟合预测器，采用bootstrap-Lasso进行特征稳定性分析，并给出线性近似残差的浓度分析。

Result: 稳定性分析识别出三个核心特征（提示级一致性扩散、标签辅助的首个正确样本位置、完成长度方差），加上熵扩展后的紧凑预测器达到Spearman ρ=0.90。

Conclusion: 该方法可用于在对候选配置支付完整奖励模型评分成本之前，通过标注验证集进行筛选。

Abstract: Best-of-$N$ inference scaling (drawing $N$ candidate answers from a language model and returning the one a reward model ranks highest) improves accuracy by an amount that varies across models, but predicting that amount in advance currently requires running the procedure end-to-end. Prior work links cheap statistics of a model's sampled outputs and validation-set correctness (how often samples agree, how diverse they are, how confident the model is, and where correct samples appear) to model behavior, but does not isolate which of these form a stable, compact predictor of best-of-$N$ gain. We fit ridge predictors on features computed from a single labeled validation-set sampling pass, use bootstrap-Lasso as a stability analysis of the candidate feature set, and give a concentration analysis with an explicit linear-approximation residual. Across three base-model families, six post-training methods, and math and reasoning task domains, the stability analysis identifies a strict three-feature core spanning prompt-level agreement spread, label-assisted first-correct-sample position, and completion-length variance; a compact ridge predictor built from this core plus an entropy add-on reaches Spearman $ρ= 0.90$ with actual best-of-$N$ gain under a reward-model verifier. The intended use is labeled validation-set screening of candidate configurations before paying the full reward-model scoring cost.

</details>


### [142] [A Locally Deployed RAG-Based Academic Advising System for Course Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02983)
*Feng Li,Yoritaka Iwata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于RAG（检索增强生成）的学术咨询系统，利用大语言模型结合结构化课程大纲数据，在本地部署且保护隐私的前提下，为学生提供课程选择、先修课程理解和个性化学习规划支持，以解决课程序列规划中的信息过载和资源不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生独立规划课程序列时面临认知局限和信息过载，容易产生困惑；同时教育机构因资源有限难以提供充分的学术建议，导致学生难以基于先修关系构建最优的课程学习路径。

Method: 提出一种本地部署的RAG（检索增强生成）学术咨询系统，将大语言模型与结构化课程大纲数据的检索相结合，以隐私保护的方式支持课程选择、先修课程理解和个性化学习规划。

Result: 系统能够有效支持学生进行课程选择、理解先修关系并制定个性化学习计划，同时满足隐私保护要求。

Conclusion: 基于RAG的本地部署学术咨询系统通过融合大语言模型与结构化课程数据，能在保护隐私的前提下缓解学生课程规划中的信息过载问题，并弥补教育机构学术指导资源不足的缺陷。

Abstract: The correct sequence of courses in the curriculum based on prerequisites between courses is of great importance for students to develop their knowledge and skills holistically. However, students crafting this sequence in isolation frequently struggle with recognition limitations and information overload that leads to confusion. Simultaneously, education institutions encounter difficulties in providing adequate academic advice for the correct sequence due to limited education resources. To address these challenges, we propose a locally deployed RAG-based academic advising system grounded in syllabus information. By combining large language models with retrieval from structured syllabus data, the system is designed to support course selection, prerequisite understanding, and personalized study planning in a privacy-preserving manner.

</details>


### [143] [Pretraining Language Models on Historical Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02991)
*Xiaoxi Luo,Zachary Shinnick,Niclas Griesshaber,Yixuan Wang,Junchi Yu,Freda Shi,Philip Torr,Yao Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TypewriterLM，一个基于1913年前英语文本训练的7.24B历史语言模型，通过构建54B token的历史语料库TypewriterCorpus、提出词汇接地指令微调框架以及History-Event基准套件，解决了数据质量、时间泄漏、时间一致性训练和评估等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发历史语言模型面临数据质量和可用性、防止时间泄漏、设计时间一致的训练后流程以及构建可靠评估等挑战，现有方法无法有效应对这些问题。

Method: 1) 构建TypewriterCorpus，包含54B token的历史语料，来自多种档案和语言学标注来源，并进行广泛的数据清洗和泄漏缓解；2) 提出词汇接地指令微调（lexically grounded instructing tuning），约束回答直接基于历史源文档；3) 构建History-LIMA和History-SelfInstruct两个历史指令微调数据集；4) 引入History-Event基准套件，评估能力、时间接地和数据泄漏。

Result: 成功训练了TypewriterLM模型并构建了配套资源，包括语料库、微调数据集和评估基准。

Conclusion: TypewriterLM及其所有相关资源已开源，以支持未来历史语言模型的研究。

Abstract: We introduce TypewriterLM, a 7.24B History language model (LM) trained exclusively on English text predating 1913. Developing History LMs requires addressing challenges in data quality and availability, preventing temporal leakage, designing temporally consistent post-training pipelines, and constructing reliable evaluations. To address these issues, we construct TypewriterCorpus, a 54B-token historical corpus collected from diverse archival and linguistically annotated sources with extensive data cleaning and leakage mitigation procedures. Furthermore, we introduce lexically grounded instructing tuning, a post-training framework that constraints responses to remain directly grounded in historical source documents. Using this framework we construct two historical instruction tuning datasets: History-LIMA and History-SelfInstruct. To evaluate capability and temporal consistency, we introduce History-Event, a benchmark suite for evaluating competence, temporal grounding and data leakage. We release TypewriterLM and all associated resources to support future research on historical language models.

</details>


### [144] [Hint-Guided Diversified Policy Optimization for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03021)
*Zhiyu Cao,Kaixin Wu,Mingjie Zhong,Peifeng Li,Xiaobo Li,Can Ye,Qiaoming Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Hint-Guided Diversified Policy Optimization (HDPO)方法，让LLM先列出多种候选方案提纲（hint），再选择最可靠的方案进行推理，通过"提议-选择-思考"的轨迹提升推理能力、解决方案多样性和可靠性识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR奖励机制仅关注结果正确性，缺乏引导模型考虑多样化解决方案的显式信号，而人类解决问题通常会评估多种方法并选择最可靠的方案，当前RLVR框架未显式激励这一认知过程。

Method: HDPO包含两个阶段：1) Cold Start for Structured Reasoning - 结构化推理冷启动；2) Hint-Guided Diversified Reinforcement Learning - 提示引导的多样化强化学习，激励模型遵循"提议-选择-思考"轨迹生成多样化且可靠的解决方案。

Result: 实验结果表明HDPO有效提升了LLM的推理能力，增强了候选解决方案的多样性，以及模型识别可靠解决方案的能力。

Conclusion: 通过显式激励模型先提出多样化候选方案再选择可靠方案的结构化推理范式，能够弥补现有RLVR框架在多样化思考方面的不足，提升LLM的推理性能和可靠性。

Abstract: Recent developments in Large Language Models (LLMs) have showcased impressive reasoning capabilities, with Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) being a promising enhancement strategy. However, existing reward mechanisms are constrained to the outcome-level correctness and lack explicit signals to guide the model to consider diverse solutions. In contrast, human problem solving typically involves evaluating multiple potential approaches and selecting the most reliable solution, a cognitive process that current RLVR frameworks do not explicitly incentivize. Inspired by this, we propose Hint-Guided Diversified Policy Optimization (HDPO), allowing the model to first list all potential candidate solution outlines as hints and then select the most reliable one for further reasoning. HDPO comprises two stages of Cold Start for Structured Reasoning and Hint-Guided Diversified Reinforcement Learning to incentivize the model to generate diverse and reliable solutions following the ``propose-select-think'' trajectory. Experimental results show that HDPO effectively boosts LLM reasoning and enhances the diversity of candidate solutions as well as the LLM's ability to identify reliable solutions.

</details>


### [145] [Hallucinations as Orthogonal Noise: Inference-Time Manifold Alignment via Dynamic Contextual Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03022)
*Mingkuan Zhao,Wentao Hu,Tianchen Huang,Yuheng Min,Suquan Chen,Yide Gao,Yanbo Zhai,Shuangyong Song,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出动态上下文正交化（DCO）方法，通过正交分解和Z-score抑制机制，在推理阶段抑制LLM的幻觉，在保持知识保留的同时提升了上下文忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）在生成内容时容易出现幻觉（hallucination），即生成与上下文事实或逻辑约束不一致的内容，这阻碍了LLM的可靠部署。现有方法在抑制幻觉与保留参数化知识之间存在权衡，需要更有效的解决方案。

Method: 基于线性表示假说（linear representation hypothesis）的几何框架，将幻觉视为残差流语义流形上的正交噪声。提出Dynamic Contextual Orthogonalization (DCO)，利用输入残差流作为动态上下文锚点，对注意力头输出进行正交分解；通过层级的Z-score抑制机制，根据统计分布选择性地削弱离群的正交成分。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B和70B上，对XSum、NQ-Swap、IFEval等基准的评估表明，DCO在上下文忠实度上优于现有干预基线方法；同时在TriviaQA和TruthfulQA等知识密集型任务上保持高性能，有效缓解了幻觉抑制与参数知识保留之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 验证了幻觉的几何解释（即幻觉是语义流形上的正交噪声），DCO作为一种计算高效的推理时干预方法，能够有效实现流形对齐（manifold alignment）。
